  MoganBert-TR attains 78.41 on TrGLUE, the best among the Turkish ModernBERT models compared, and 77.73 on TabiBench, where it leads two of the eight categories with the largest margin on code retrieval (+3.62 points over TabiBERT). MoganBert-Embed, produced through teacher distillation and multi-signal contrastive fine-tuning, ranks first among student models on the MTEB(Turkish) overall average with 68.30 and reaches 99.5% of its 7.57B-parameter teacher's score with a 51x smaller backbone. The accompanying 50,048-token tokenizer outperforms all compared Turkish tokenizers on compression and fertility across two independent test sets. Weights, tokenizer, embedding model and evaluation code: https://huggingface.co/moganai

</details>


### [143] [Localize-Then-Decide Guarantees for LLM Judgments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25824)
*Xinyu Li,Yi Zhou,Guanqun Cao,Zeyu Fu,Tianjin Huang,Gaojie Jin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出“先定位后决策”框架，通过共形预测缩小候选范围并校准置信度，解决大语言模型在多选场景下置信度与人类判断不一致的问题，实现高概率的一致性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于置信度阈值的方法假设高置信度意味着低分歧风险，但在候选响应数量增加时，概率质量分布会扭曲置信度估计，导致该假设失效，难以提供可靠的人类判断一致性保证。

Method: 提出“Localize-Then-Decide”框架：首先利用共形预测将候选集缩小为包含人类偏好响应的高概率短列表；随后应用校准后的基于置信度的规则从短列表中选择一个响应或弃权，以恢复置信度与分歧风险之间的单调关系。

Result: 在不同候选规模、数据集和评判LLM的实验表明，该框架相比单阶段基线方法， consistently 实现了更高的保证成功率和显著更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 通过分阶段策略有效解决了多候选场景下的置信度失真问题，为大语言模型作为评估器提供了更可靠的一致性保证机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used as evaluators to assess output quality and preference alignment, yet providing reliable guarantees of agreement with human judgments remains challenging. Recent work introduces confidence-thresholding methods that provide such guarantees for pairwise comparisons, relying on the assumption that higher estimated confidence implies lower disagreement risk with humans. However, this assumption can break down when the number of candidate responses increases, since distributing probability mass across many alternatives can distort confidence estimates. To address this issue, we propose a Localize-Then-Decide framework. First, conformal prediction localizes a small shortlist that contains the human-preferred response with high probability. Then, a calibrated confidence-based rule selectively chooses a single response from this shortlist or abstains. This design restores the monotonic relationship between confidence and disagreement risk and enables high-probability agreement guarantees. Experiments with multiple candidate sizes across several datasets and judge LLMs demonstrate that our framework consistently achieves higher guarantee success rates and substantially higher coverage than single-stage baselines.

</details>


### [144] [Unfolding Scientific Papers into Multi-Turn Generation Trajectories for Continued Pre-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25826)
*Qiankai Xu,Qiguang Chen,Zixin Su,Wenhao Huang,Yue Gao,Jiaheng Liu,Ge Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种将科学论文解构为多轮生成轨迹（写作请求、全局计划、节间预写思考）的流水线，构建大规模预训练和微调数据集，显著提升模型学术写作能力并增强长文档阅读能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成数据工作仅针对短文本段落进行局部思维重构，未触及文档整体结构；科学论文具有清晰统一的结构，适合将此范式扩展至文档级别，以解决长文档写作与理解问题。

Method: 构建流水线，对arXiv论文进行逆向工程：保留原文章节和摘要，由教师模型重建“写作请求-全局计划-各节预写 deliberation”的多步轨迹。基于此生成用于继续预训练（CPT）的语料库（约为源文本两倍大小）、监督微调（SFT）数据集及PAW-Bench学术写作基准。

Result: 在CPT后接SFT的实验显示，该方法广泛提升了写作基准表现，同时保持通用推理能力并改善长文档阅读理解。即使使用专用写作SFT数据集，该增益依然持续；混合本文SFT数据可进一步提升学术写作水平。

Conclusion: 利用科学论文的结构性特征进行文档级别的思维链合成，是提升大模型学术写作、长文档处理及通用推理能力的有效途径。

Abstract: A recent line of synthetic-data work reconstructs the thinking behind existing text rather than rewriting the text itself, but it operates on short web passages, recovers only local thoughts, and leaves the structure of whole documents untouched. Scientific papers are written to a clear and largely uniform structure and make a natural substrate for lifting this paradigm to the document level. We present a pipeline that unfolds each paper into a multi-turn generation trajectory in which a teacher model reconstructs the writing process of the whole paper: a writing request, a global plan, and pre-writing deliberation for each section. All section texts and the abstract are kept verbatim from the source paper. We apply the pipeline to quality-filtered arXiv papers and obtain a corpus for continued pre-training (CPT) that is roughly twice the size of the source text. The same reverse construction extends to instruction data and evaluation. Treating real paper text as the answer yields an SFT dataset. Anchoring tasks in held-out papers yields PAW-Bench, an academic-writing benchmark whose tasks carry their own rubrics and checklists. In controlled experiments CPT on our corpus followed by supervised fine-tuning on public datasets improves writing benchmarks broadly while preserving general reasoning and improving long-document reading. The writing gain persists even when every model is fine-tuned on a dedicated writing SFT dataset. Mixing our SFT data into that recipe lifts academic writing further.

</details>


### [145] [Skill Issue: Are Skills Language-Invariant in LLMs?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25832)
*Bobby Cheng,Adam Gaber,Zhengyuan Liu,Catherine Arnett,Omer Goldman,Cheston Tan,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过多语言自我对弈实验，量化了大语言模型在不同语言下的技能不一致性。研究发现同一模型在不同语言下表现差异显著，且改变中间推理语言可部分恢复性能，表明语言能力是阻碍真正多模态模型发展的主要障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大型语言模型在跨语言交互中，除了知识获取外，其技能（如推理、决策）是否存在不一致性，并量化这种差异。

Method: 构建基于TextArena的多语言扩展框架，采用“多语言自我对弈”方法：同一模型的两个实例通过不同语言界面在同一文本游戏中竞争。控制模型、对手、规则等变量不变，仅改变语言接口。评估三个开源模型在八种语言和六种游戏（涵盖空间推理、不完全信息、资源分配等）中的表现。

Result: 1. 同一模型在不同语言下的竞技强度存在显著差异，体现在胜率、无效动作频率和战略倾向的系统性变化上。2. 发现特定于语言的失败模式，如空间推理、卡牌条件决策和最优移动选择方面的缺陷。3. 在某些场景下，仅更改中间推理语言即可大幅恢复丢失的性能，表明语言影响决策过程的不同阶段。

Conclusion: 技能差异是多语言模型开发中的重大可测量障碍。深入理解这些差异有助于设计在各语言间表现更公平、更均衡的模型。

Abstract: Large language models access knowledge inconsistently across languages, but to what extent do they differ in their skill sets when interacting with different languages? This work quantifies cross-lingual skill inconsistency orthogonally from knowledge and general benchmark performance. We do this via multilingual self-play: two instances of the same model compete in a text-based game, each interacting through a different language interface. Since the model, opponent, rules, state space, and available actions remain fixed, this setting isolates the effect of language on the model's realized behavior. We build a multilingual extension to TextArena and evaluate three open-weight models across eight languages and six games covering spatial reasoning, imperfect information, resource allocation, and repeated interaction. We find that the same model can exhibit markedly different playing strength across languages, with systematic variation in win--loss margins, invalid actions, and strategic tendencies. Detailed analyses reveal language-specific failures in spatial reasoning, card-conditioned decisions, and optimal move selection. In some settings, changing only the intermediate reasoning language recovers much of the lost performance, suggesting that language can affect different stages of the decision process. These results show that skill discrepancies are a measurable major roadblock in the development of truly multilingual models. Better understanding these discrepancies can help us design models that perform more equitably across languages.

</details>


### [146] [Key Point Analysis Needs Structure Recovery: Task Definition, Dataset Diagnosis, and a Structure-Aware Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25854)
*Zhiqiang Shi,Oana Cocarascu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出关键要点分析（KPA）本质上是结构化预测问题，现有基准存在质量缺陷。为此，作者通过人机协作重新标注构建了一个更高质量的结构感知基准，并发布了相关资源以推动未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KPA基准在分组质量、冗余度、覆盖率和论点-要点映射方面存在局限性，导致基于参考的评估出现天花板效应和选择失败，亟需改进以支持真正的KPA研究。

Method: 将KPA建模为结构化预测问题，包含恢复语义分组、生成代表性要点、确保覆盖率和估计流行度四个要素；采用人工参与的重标注方法构建结构感知且对分布敏感的基准。

Result: 新基准在语义分组一致性、要点质量、覆盖率及流行度估计可靠性上均优于现有标注；同时发布了多项注释资源，支持KPA评估、匹配、可解释性及LLM-as-a-judge等研究方向。

Conclusion: 提出了面向真正KPA的研究议程，并通过高质量基准和资源为后续研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Key Point Analysis (KPA) aims to identify a concise set of key points that summarize a collection of arguments together with their prevalence. We argue that KPA is fundamentally a structured prediction problem that requires recovering semantic groupings, generating representative key points, ensuring coverage, and estimating prevalence. Under this formulation, we show that existing KPA benchmarks suffer from limitations in grouping quality, redundancy, coverage, and argument-key point mappings, causing ceiling violation and selection failure in reference-based evaluation. To support future research on true KPA, we introduce a structure-aware, distribution-sensitive benchmark built via a human-in-the-loop re-annotation. Human and LLM evaluations consistently show that the resulting structures yield more coherent groupings, higher-quality key points, better coverage, and more reliable prevalence estimates than existing annotations. We further release several annotation resources to support research on KPA evaluation, argument-key point matching, explainable KPA, and LLM-as-a-judge methodologies, and outline a research agenda for true KPA.

</details>


### [147] [Anchoring Bias in LLM-as-a-Judge Systems: Prior Scores Compromise Evaluation Independence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25869)
*Ante Kapetanovic,Kemal Altwlkany,Andro Mercep,Tomislav Duricic,Emanuel Lacic*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM-as-a-Judge范式存在严重的“锚定效应”，先前的评分即使仅作为元数据上下文，也会系统性地扭曲后续判断。该偏差无法通过思维链或简单警告消除，需重视上下文工程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证LLM-as-a-Judge范式中“每次评估相互独立”的假设是否成立，探究先前评分（作为元数据）是否会对当前评估产生系统性偏差。

Method: 通过三种提示条件（无元数据、修订框架、包含修订/尝试次数/先前评分的锚定元数据），对192,000次评估进行实验；使用Cohen's d衡量分布差异，并通过Token级分析探索响应模式；在带有真实标签的行业分类数据上验证偏差影响。

Result: 7/8模型显示显著的锚定效应（Bootstrap区间低于零，Cohen's d绝对值达0.71）。锚定元数据导致48%的错误修正被阻断，10.18%的正确判断被翻转。思维链和忽略元数据的警告均未能完全消除总效应。

Conclusion: LLM评估并非 impartial（公正无私），而是受上下文强烈影响。可靠的LLM评估需要精细的上下文工程设计，且缓解策略必须针对特定模型和任务域进行验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly assess generated content, giving rise to the LLM-as-a-Judge paradigm. These systems now score outputs, filter content, and gate iterative refinement in production pipelines, where each judgment is often assumed to be independent of earlier evaluations. We test this assumption using three prompt conditions: no metadata, revision framing, and anchored metadata containing revision, attempt, and prior-score fields. We show that prior scores, even when included only as context metadata, anchor judgments and systematically shift ratings toward their values. Across 192,000 attempted evaluations (185,271 successful), seven out of the eight evaluated models have 95% task-stratified bootstrap intervals below zero for the total anchored-metadata effect on 20 fixed texts. Cohen's $d$, a standardized measure of the difference between score distributions, reaches an absolute value of 0.71. Token-level analysis of selected model-task probes suggests a threshold-like response pattern: introducing anchored metadata produces a marked redistribution of output-score probabilities, while changing the anchor value within the tested below-threshold range produces comparatively little additional variation. On categorical industry data with human-labeled ground truth, anchored metadata blocks 48% of error corrections and flips 10.18% of correct judgments toward an assigned wrong label, demonstrating the bias extends beyond numerical scoring to categorical decisions. Neither Chain-of-Thought nor a metadata-disregard warning reduces the total effect, although the warning improves the paired accuracy effect relative to baseline in the industry experiment. Reliable LLM evaluation demands careful context engineering rather than an assumption of impartiality. Effective mitigation must be validated for the intended model and task or domain.

</details>


### [148] [Loss-Based Active Learning for Neural Abstractive Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25881)
*Michail Ioannou,Tatiana Passali,George Michalopoulos,Grigorios Tsoumakas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出LOBSTER框架，通过基于损失和语义相似度的主动学习策略，高效解决摘要模型微调中数据标注成本高及现有方法计算瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要模型微调依赖高质量标注数据，获取成本高且耗时；现有主动学习方法在摘要任务中存在不稳定性高、计算开销大的瓶颈。

Method: 提出LOBSTER（Loss-Based Active leaRning）框架，优先选择与当前高损失训练样本语义相似的未标注实例进行标注，以针对性修正模型弱点。

Result: 在三个基准数据集和两个骨干模型上评估，LOBSTER性能匹配或优于当前最先进方法，且查询选择速度提升最高达665倍。

Conclusion: LOBSTER有效克服了主动学习在摘要应用中的挑战，实现了高性能与高效率的平衡。

Abstract: Fine-tuning abstractive summarization models requires high-quality annotated data. However, obtaining such corpora is expensive and time-consuming, as it requires human annotators to read and comprehend long documents to create accurate summaries. Active learning mitigates this issue by selecting only the most informative instances for annotation, allowing models to achieve competitive results with significantly fewer labels. However, the application of active learning to summarization remains under-explored, and existing studies often suffer from instability and significant computational bottlenecks. To overcome these challenges, we propose LOBSTER (LOss-BaSed acTivE leaRning), a novel active learning framework designed specifically for abstractive summarization. LOBSTER improves performance by prioritizing unlabeled instances semantically similar to the model's current high-loss training examples, enabling the model to explicitly correct its specific weaknesses. Our empirical evaluation across three benchmark datasets and two summarization backbone models demonstrates that LOBSTER consistently matches or outperforms current state-of-the-art approaches while achieving a query selection speedup of up to 665x.

</details>


### [149] [From Passive Response to Proactive Correction: Enhancing LLM Robustness Against Input Fact Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25894)
*Ping Wang,Xiangguo Sun,Bingbing Xu,Guocong Li,Xiaofeng Meng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DEDUCE框架，通过检测、策略制定和纠正三个阶段，将LLM从被动响应者转变为主动纠错者，以应对包含误导性前提的用户输入导致的幻觉问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉缓解方法通常假设用户输入可靠，忽视了事实性错误如何主动误导模型推理；LLM在面对包含误导性前提的输入时，常产生自信但事实错误的回答。

Method: 提出DEDUCE三阶段框架：(1) 通过细粒度事实提取和验证检测错误；(2) 通过多视角审议制定纠正策略；(3) 纠正误解并提供可靠答案。同时发布MisFactQA数据集及新评估指标。

Result: 在TruthfulQA、FalseQA和MisFactQA基准上的实验表明，DEDUCE显著提高了准确性和纠错能力；在Qwen、LLaMA和Gemma系列模型上的一致增益证实了其有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: DEDUCE框架能有效提升LLM在处理包含事实性错误输入时的鲁棒性和准确性，具有广泛的适用性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) frequently produce confident yet factually incorrect responses when user inputs contain misleading premises, a phenomenon we attribute to fact perturbations in the input. Existing approaches to hallucination mitigation typically assume reliable user inputs, overlooking how such factual errors can actively mislead model reasoning. To address this vulnerability, we propose DEDUCE, a three-stage framework that transforms LLMs from passive responders into proactive error correctors. DEDUCE operates in three stages: (1) detect errors through fine-grained fact extraction and verification; (2) devise correction strategies via multi perspective deliberation; and (3) correct misconceptions while delivering reliable answers. We also present MisFactQA, a dataset containing factual errors of varying degrees, and propose new metrics for evaluating model robustness. Experiments on TruthfulQA, FalseQA, and our MisFactQA benchmark demonstrate that DEDUCE significantly improves both accuracy and error correction capability. Consistent gains across Qwen, LLaMA, and Gemma families confirm its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [150] [One Form to Transfer Them All: Pretraining Multilingual Language Models Beyond Native Orthography](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25904)
*Muge Zhang,Aaron Jencks,Krishna Badikela,Yulia Tsvetkov,Sachin Kumar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究对比了自回归多语言预训练中不同输入表示（正交文本、IPA、罗马化）的效果，发现罗马化在跨语言迁移上表现最佳，且优势随模型规模扩大而增加。建议将罗马化作为预训练阶段的核心设计选择，而非事后补救措施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多关注编码器-only模型或针对现有预训练模型的适配，缺乏对自回归多语言预训练中不同输入表示的系统性直接比较，特别是在书写系统不同的相关语言之间。

Method: 在三种模型规模（467M, 709M, 1.03B）和八种语言（分为四组类型学配对）的控制设置下，系统性比较了正交文本、国际音标（IPA）和罗马化在自回归多语言预训练中的表现，并在多种下游任务上进行评估。

Result: 1. 罗马化预训练产生最强的跨语言迁移能力，且其相对于文本的优势随模型规模扩大而显著增加。2. IPA在大多数设置下优于文本，但落后于罗马化。3. 令人惊讶的是，在已覆盖脚本的语言上，对文本预训练模型进行罗马化数据微调会损害性能；仅在模型缺乏脚本覆盖时才有边际帮助。

Conclusion: 对于跨越类型学多样书写系统的多语言模型，为了获得最大收益，应将罗马化视为在预训练阶段应用的核心设计选择，而不是事后修复手段。

Abstract: Multilingual language models transfer knowledge across languages through shared subword vocabulary, a mechanism that breaks down when related languages use different writing systems. Prior work addresses this via script equalization (romanization or IPA transcription), but direct comparisons are rare; the focus has been on encoder-only models, with most work adapting existing pretrained models. We systematically compare different input representations in autoregressive multilingual pretraining, comparing orthographic text, IPA, and romanization in a controlled setup across three scales (467M, 709M, and 1.03B) on eight languages in four typologically motivated pairs. Across a wide range of downstream tasks on seen and unseen languages, romanized pretraining yields the strongest cross-lingual transfer, and the advantage over text widens with scale. IPA improves over text in most settings but trails romanization. Surprisingly, finetuning a text-pretrained model on romanized data hurts performance on languages already covered by the base model, only marginally helping when the model lacks script coverage. Our results indicate that for multilingual models spanning typologically diverse scripts, to obtain maximum benefits, romanization should be treated as a core design choice applied at pretraining rather than a post hoc fix.

</details>


### [151] [SAMpLE: A SystemC-AMS Machine LEarning-based Framework for Virtual Prototyping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25910)
*Andrei Mihai Albu,Sara Vinco*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SAMpLE，一个基于SystemC-AMS的开源框架，通过标准化接口将机器学习模型作为Timed Dataflow组件集成到嵌入式系统虚拟原型中，支持ONNX格式导入和两种执行后端（在线训练与离线执行），旨在解决现有集成方案复用性差、不可比、不可复现的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习模型在嵌入式系统虚拟原型中的应用多依赖临时性（ad hoc）解决方案，导致代码复用性低、结果缺乏可比性和可复现性。

Method: 开发SAMpLE框架，利用SystemC-AMS平台，通过标准化即插即用接口将ML模型转化为TDF组件；采用ONNX作为标准模型交换格式以集成外部训练的模型；提供原生C++后端用于轻量级模型在线训练，以及离线后端用于直接执行外部开发模型，无需重新实现或手动集成。

Result: 实现了模块化设计，允许在同一测试台、数据集和仿真工作流中评估不同的ML解决方案；建立了统一且可复现的环境，支持在不修改SystemC-AMS结构的前提下扩展新模型。

Conclusion: SAMpLE通过标准化和模块化方法提升了ML模型在虚拟平台仿真中的集成效率、复用性及研究的可复现性，为未来扩展提供了灵活基础。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) is increasingly used in virtual prototypes of embedded systems to model behaviors that are difficult to capture analytically. However, integrating ML models into virtual platform simulation is still typically done through ad hoc solutions, which limits reuse, comparability, and reproducibility. This paper presents \textbf{\textit{SAMpLE}}, an open-source SystemC-AMS-based framework that integrates ML models as first-class Timed Dataflow (TDF) components through a standardized plug-and-play interface. SAMpLE provides two execution backends: a native C++ backend for online training of lightweight models, and an offline backend for executing externally developed models without requiring re-implementation in C++ or manual integration steps. The framework uses ONNX as a standard model exchange format to enable integration of externally trained ML models into SystemC-AMS simulations, and allows the evaluation of different ML-based solutions within the same testbench, dataset, and simulation workflow. The modular design and unified and reproducible environment will allow future extensions of SAMpLE to new models, without modifying the SystemC-AMS structure.

</details>


### [152] [Unveiling Spectral Mechanisms in Training-Free LLM Text Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25944)
*Haitong Luo,Xuying Meng,Weiyao Zhang,Wenji Zou,Shengfeng Lou,Xuefeng Jiang,Chungang Lin,Yujun Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文从理论和实证角度分析基于频谱的大语言模型（LLM）检测机制，揭示其核心在于捕捉人类写作特有的“生成活力”（信号波动），并明确了该方法在长文本、连续生成场景下效果最佳，而在短文本或混合编辑场景中需结合其他指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大型语言模型的发展，区分人类写作与机器生成文本变得困难。现有的免训练检测方法主要依赖平均token概率，往往忽略了体现人类写作特征的“生成活力”（即信号波动）。尽管频谱分析能捕捉这种活力，但其作用机制及适用边界尚不明确。

Method: 作者从理论推导和经验验证两个维度对频谱检测进行了深入分析。理论上，将频谱能量与代理对数概率轨迹的方差联系起来，解释了更广泛的人类token选择如何产生频域指标所需的波动；经验上，评估了不同文本长度和采样范围对检测信号强度的影响。

Result: 研究发现频谱信号的强度取决于文本长度和采样范围：在长篇幅、连续且受限的生成场景中，频谱证据最为清晰；而在短文本、碎片化、混合或经过编辑的场景中，单一频谱方法效果有限，需要结合置信度分析和波动视图进行互补。

Conclusion: 本研究阐明了频域检测的有效时机，为未来设计多维度的检测器提供了理论依据和实践指导，特别是在处理复杂生成场景时，应综合使用多种检测视角。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Language Models (LLMs) makes it increasingly difficult to distinguish human writing from machine-generated text. Training-free detection offers a scalable solution, yet common confidence-based metrics mainly measure average token probabilities and often miss the signal fluctuations that characterize human writing, which we call "generative vitality". Spectral analysis offers a way to capture this vitality, but its mechanism and practical boundaries remain underexplored. In this paper, we analyze spectral detection from both theoretical and empirical perspectives. We connect spectral energy to variance in proxy log-probability trajectories and explain how broader human token choices create the fluctuations used by frequency-domain indicators. We further show that the strength of this signal depends on text length and sampling range: spectral evidence is clearest for long, continuous, constrained generation, while short, fragmented, mixed, and edited settings require complementary confidence and fluctuation views. These findings clarify when frequency-domain detection works and provide guidance for future multi-dimensional detector design.

</details>


### [153] [Lost but not erased: Finding traces of a forgotten language in neural speech models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25976)
*Peter Plantinga,Charlotte Moore,Peter W. Donhauser,Krista Byers-Heinlein,Denise Klein*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究通过自动语音识别模型模拟国际收养者的语言学习过程，发现第一语言的音系痕迹主要保留在底层非音位表征中。这些痕迹具有功能性优势（加速再学习），表明关键期效应源于基础表征的固化（entrenchment）而非神经可塑性的成熟性丧失，强调经验在语言习得中的核心作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战将国际收养者保留出生语言音系痕迹的现象单纯归因于生物定时关键期（biologically-timed critical period）的传统观点，探讨这是否可能反映普通的学习动力学，且不受成熟度混淆因素的影响。

Method: 使用自动语音识别（ASR）模型模拟国际收养者体验：先在一个语言上训练，然后突然切换到第二种语言进行训练，以此排除生理成熟带来的混淆变量。

Result: 1. 第一语言的痕迹在第二语言训练期间持续存在，但主要集中在最低层的预音位（pre-phonemic）层。2. 这些痕迹是功能性的：早期暴露的模型比无经验的模型快14%重新学会丢失的第一语言。3. 这种优势在与从相关语言早期收养的模型对比时依然存在，但在用非收养模型的早期层替换后消失。

Conclusion: 关键期效应反映的是基础表征的固化（entrenchment of foundational representations），而非成熟导致的可塑性丧失；经验在语言习得的关键期中起着中心作用。

Abstract: International adoptees retain phonological traces of a birth language they can no longer speak or comprehend, a persistence typically attributed to a biologically-timed critical period. We asked whether it could instead reflect the ordinary dynamics of learning, using automatic speech recognition models that simulate the international adoptee experience without maturational confounds. Models were trained on one language and then abruptly switched to a second. We found that traces of the first language persisted throughout second-language training, but mainly in the lowest, pre-phonemic layers. These traces were functional, as models with early exposure re-learned their lost first language 14% faster than naive models; this advantage held even against models adopted early from a related language and disappeared when the earliest layers were substituted from a non-adopted model. We argue that these critical-period effects reflect entrenchment of foundational representations rather than a maturational loss of plasticity, and that experience plays a central role in critical periods in language acquisition.

</details>


### [154] [When Personality Meets Quantization: A Layer-wise MBTI Analysis of Quantized LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25977)
*Yao Fu,Lijia Huang,Xiaomin Li,Runchao Li,Yu Yin,Kenneth A. Loparo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究系统分析了量化大语言模型（LLM）的MBTI人格特征，发现人格是受量化、提示和解码影响的动态涌现过程，而非静态属性。4-bit量化基本保留人格结构，而2-bit显著破坏一致性；人格决策主要在上层形成，且推理解码会引发人格漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注全精度模型且仅评估最终输出，忽视了广泛部署的低内存占用量化LLM的人格特性及其内部机制，导致量化模型的人格可靠性未被充分探索。

Method: 对开源LLM进行跨精度的系统性MBTI分析，涵盖主流4-bit方法（GPTQ, AWQ）及极端2-bit设置（AQLM变体）。通过选项级熵和置信度差距动力学考察人格在层间的涌现，并引入不确定性放大层解码（UALD）以研究推理时的人格漂移。

Result: 1. ENFJ类型在不同模型族和精度中占主导；2. 4-bit量化大致保留粗略人格结构，但2-bit量化破坏了细粒度提示一致性和跨精度一致性；3. 人格决策在上层形成，早期层存在大量模糊性；4. 推理解码可改变人格，而与人格对齐的条件化可提高鲁棒性。

Conclusion: LLM的人格是依赖层的动态决策过程，受量化和推理策略敏感影响。这为量化LLM的行为可靠性提供了新视角，强调在人格敏感型聊天机器人应用中需考虑内部动力学和推理策略。

Abstract: Personality is increasingly important in large language models (LLMs), as it shapes users' trust, engagement, and emotional experiences. While the Myers--Briggs Type Indicator (MBTI) has emerged as a common framework for assessing LLMs' personality, existing studies focus primarily on full-precision models and evaluate only final outputs. They overlook the widespread deployment of quantized LLMs requiring low memory footprints, whose personality traits remain underexplored. In this work, we present a systematic MBTI analysis of open-source LLMs across multiple precisions, including mainstream 4-bit methods (GPTQ, AWQ) and extreme 2-bit settings (AQLM variants). Beyond output-level evaluation, we examine how personality emerges across layers through option-level entropy and confidence-gap dynamics, and introduce Uncertainty-Amplified Layer Decoding (UALD) to study decoding-induced personality drift at inference time. Our results reveal a key insight: LLMs' personality is not a static property, but an emergent, layer-dependent decision process sensitive to quantization, prompting, and decoding. Specifically, we find that (1) ENFJ remains dominant across model families and precisions; (2) 4-bit quantization largely preserves coarse personality structure, while 2-bit quantization disrupts fine-grained prompt consistency and cross-precision agreement; (3) personality decisions emerges in upper layers, following substantial ambiguity in early layers; and (4) inference decoding can shift personality, while personality-aligned conditioning improves robustness. These findings provide a new perspective on the behavioral reliability of quantized LLMs and highlight the importance of considering internal dynamics and inference strategies in personality-sensitive chatbot applications.

</details>


### [155] [Distinct dynamics of conceptual and referential disruptions in human reading and large language model processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25999)
*Rui He,Nihal Altay,Wolfram Hinzen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究对比了概念意义与指称意义在人类阅读及大语言模型中的处理动态。发现概念干扰导致局部集中且快速衰减的处理成本，而指称干扰引发更分散、持续的下游影响，两者处理机制存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 考察语义的两个维度——概念内容与指称实体——在话语进入时的处理动力学特征，并探究人类认知与大语言模型（LLM）在这些过程中的异同。

Method: 通过短篇叙事中选择性破坏概念或指称信息，追踪其对人类自步速阅读的影响；同时分析大语言模型在处理此类干扰时的预测误差（surprisal）和输出层表示变化。

Result: 人类阅读中，概念干扰产生强烈但局部的即时加工成本并迅速衰减；指称干扰效应较弱、随后续词汇逐渐减弱且受句子边界调节更强。LLM中，两种干扰均在被操作词处立即显现：上下文 surprisal 模式与人类阅读高度平行（概念干扰局部集中且快速衰减，指称干扰较小且渐进）；但输出层表示显示指称干扰引起更大的初始位移，随后两者均呈幂律衰减。

Conclusion: 概念信息与指称信息具有可区分处理动力学：概念信息施加更局部的整合成本，而指称信息涉及维持话语层面身份感的更分布式处理过程。

Abstract: Linguistic meaning is grounded in conceptual content, from which reference to particular entities emerges as words enter discourse. To examine the processing dynamics associated with these two dimensions of meaning, we selectively disrupted conceptual or referential information in short narratives and traced the resulting effects in human self-paced reading and in the predictive and representational processing of large language models. In human reading, conceptual disruptions produced a strong but localized processing cost, emerging immediately after the distorted word, reaching an early maximum, and then declining rapidly. Referential disruptions produced weaker effects, which decreased more gradually across subsequent words, and were more strongly modulated by sentence boundaries. In the language model, both disruptions emerged immediately at the manipulated word. Contextual model surprisal showed a pattern closely paralleling human reading: conceptual disruption produced a larger, more locally concentrated effect that decayed rapidly, whereas referential disruption produced a smaller and more gradual downstream effect. Output-layer representations showed a different pattern: referential disruption produced a larger initial displacement, while both distortions were subsequently characterized by power-law decay. Together, these results provide convergent evidence for distinguishable processing dynamics of two types of meaning: conceptual information imposes a more locally concentrated integration cost, whereas referential information engages a more distributed process of maintaining discourse-level identity.

</details>


### [156] [VISA: Agentic Self-Evolving Data Synthesis for Multimodal Instruction Following](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26013)
*Min Zeng,Guanxin Tan,Libin Cen,Yawei Wen,Rui Hu,Liuyang Bian,Xiaolong Chen,Xiaoxin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VISA是一种基于智能体框架的多模态指令合成方法，通过自我进化的循环机制，利用失败样本的诊断恢复和记忆反馈，持续优化指令生成的多样性、难度和可验证性，从而提升多模态模型的指令遵循能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多模态指令合成流水线通常采用“一次生成并过滤”的模式，忽略了失败样本的反馈、验证器结果和目标模型错误等信息，导致数据质量受限且缺乏迭代优化能力。

Method: 提出VISA（Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent），将多模态指令合成为自我进化循环：1）分析图像以筛选不兼容约束并发现新约束；2）从持久记忆中采样多样性和难度感知的约束集；3）生成候选指令并通过可执行工具和结构化LLM裁判进行验证；4）失败样本触发诊断引导的恢复，成功样本通过目标模型评估难度；5）将验证信号和模型失败画像写回记忆，用于后续轮次的自适应调整。

Result: 在MM-IFEval基准测试中，VISA consistently提升了多模态指令遵循性能，优于强基线模型；同时在七个公共基准上保持了通用的多模态能力。此外，其验证器信号可直接作为强化学习的奖励信号，无需单独训练奖励模型。

Conclusion: VISA通过构建自我进化的闭环系统，有效解决了传统合成方法中信息利用率低的问题，显著增强了多模态模型的指令跟随能力，同时避免了额外奖励模型训练的开销，具有高效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Multimodal instruction-following models require training data that is accurate, diverse, verifiable, and challenging. Existing synthesis pipelines typically follow a one-pass generate-and-filter paradigm, discarding feedback from failed samples, verifier outcomes, and target-model errors. We present VISA (Visual Instruction Synthesis Agent), an agentic framework that reformulates multimodal instruction synthesis as a self-evolving loop. At each round, VISA analyzes an image to filter incompatible constraints and discover new verifiable ones, samples diversity- and difficulty-aware constraint sets from persistent memory, generates candidate instructions, and verifies the resulting samples with executable tools and structured large language model judges. Failed samples trigger diagnostic-guided recovery, while accepted samples are probed against the target model to estimate difficulty. The resulting verifier signals and target-model failure profiles are written back to memory, allowing subsequent rounds to adaptively expand the constraint space, reduce template repetition, and focus on unresolved model weaknesses. The same verifier contracts further provide reward signals for reinforcement learning without a separately trained reward model. Experiments on MM-IFEval show that VISA consistently improves multimodal instruction following over strong baselines, while preserving general multimodal capability across seven public benchmarks.

</details>


### [157] [Beyond Local Surprise: Grounded Dialogue as Selective Belief Revision under Referential Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26035)
*Ziming Liu,Bhanu Chaitanya Jasti,Ziyang Xu,Hongyu Wu,Yi Wu,Jiqun Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究提出了一种对话中“保留/修正”理解的框架，发现听众倾向于在局部不匹配时保持原有理解，而在累积不确定性增加时才进行修正，这一模式符合概念契约理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当说话者描述听者无法直接看到的场景时，听者如何决定是维持当前理解还是根据新话语进行修订的问题；探究对话理解是否比语言系统更保守，即相对于先前的理解来解释不匹配证据，而非立即修订。

Method: 引入一个受控的、数据驱动的框架，用于逐轮决策（保留或修订），并在相同条件下学习不同的修订策略；比较四种基于理论的修订策略，这些策略反映了对听众何时应保留或修订的不同假设。

Result: 1. 仅基于局部差异的修订政策虽然对不匹配反应强烈，但会导致 grounding 不稳定并降低检索性能。2. 基于不确定性的政策通过累积证据扩展了基于不匹配的更新，在保持连贯理解的同时维持了强大的检索性能。3. 连贯理解呈现出反直觉的模式：局部不匹配促进保留，而累积的不确定性促进修订。

Conclusion: 听众倾向于在存在局部不匹配的情况下维持先前的理解，仅在不确定性充分累积时才进行修订，这一行为模式与概念契约理论（conceptual pact theory）一致。

Abstract: When a speaker refers to a scene that the listener cannot directly see, the listener must decide whether to preserve its current understanding or revise it as new utterances arrive. Many language systems treat local mismatch as a cue for updating: divergence from the current understanding encourages adjustment. Yet conversational understanding may be more conservative, interpreting mismatching evidence relative to prior understanding rather than immediately revising it. We introduce a controlled, data-driven framework for turn-by-turn preserve/revise decisions in dialogue, where competing revision policies are learned under otherwise identical conditions. We compare four theory-driven revision strategies, each reflecting a different assumption about when listeners should preserve or revise. Two findings stand out. First, a mismatch-driven policy that updates solely based on local divergence reacts strongly to mismatch but destabilizes grounding and degrades retrieval. Second, an uncertainty-sensitive policy extends mismatch-based updating with accumulated evidence, preserving coherent understanding while maintaining strong retrieval performance. Surprisingly, coherent understanding emerges from a counterintuitive pattern: local mismatch promotes preservation, whereas accumulated uncertainty promotes revision, suggesting that listeners maintain prior understanding despite local mismatch and revise only when uncertainty sufficiently accumulates. This pattern is consistent with conceptual pact theory.

</details>


### [158] [Fine-Tuning Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa: A Preliminary Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26060)
*Leonardo Duart,Tiago Fonseca,Thiago Chacón*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究展示了如何将大型多语言基础模型Whisper微调以适应极低资源的原住民语言Baniwa的自动语音识别（ASR），在仅0.54小时语料下取得了WER 37.5%和CER 7.45%的成果，证明了该路径的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管ASR技术在高资源语言上表现优异，但原住民语言因缺乏语音资源和相关技术而受到忽视，亟需探索适用于极低资源场景的解决方案。

Method: 基于来自语言学记录项目的1,373条手动转录录音（约0.54小时，主要为孤立词和简短诱导语句），对Whisper Small模型进行监督学习微调，并使用字错误率（WER）和字符错误率（CER）进行评估。

Result: 最佳模型的WER为37.5%，CER为7.45%，证实了多语言基础模型可以成功适配到极低资源的原住民语言中。

Conclusion: 研究建立了Baniwa ASR的初始基线，并为未来涉及更大数据集、特定语言适应策略及后处理技术的研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Automatic Speech Recognition (ASR) technologies have achieved remarkable performance in recent years through the use of large multilingual foundation models. However, most advances remain concentrated on high-resource languages, while indigenous languages continue to suffer from a lack of speech resources and language technologies. This work presents a preliminary study on the adaptation of Whisper for Automatic Speech Recognition in Baniwa, an indigenous Arawakan language spoken in Brazil, Colombia, and Venezuela. The experiments were conducted using a corpus of 1,373 manually transcribed recordings obtained from a linguistic documentation project. The corpus contains approximately 0.54 hours of speech and consists primarily of isolated words and short elicited utterances. The Whisper Small model was fine-tuned using supervised learning and evaluated using Word Error Rate (WER) and Character Error Rate (CER). The best model achieved a WER of 37.5% and a CER of 7.45%, demonstrating that multilingual foundation models can be successfully adapted to extremely low-resource indigenous languages. The results establish an initial baseline for Baniwa Automatic Speech Recognition and provide a foundation for future research involving larger datasets, language-specific adaptation strategies, and post-processing techniques.

</details>


### [159] [Prefix Sliding for efficient test-time scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.26070)
*Niklas Muennighoff,Zhengyang Wang,Zeyi Chen,Weijia Shi,Binyuan Hui,John Yang,Dapeng Jiang,Mika Senghaas,Fares Obeid,Johannes Hagemann,Sami Jaghouar,Ludwig Schmidt,Percy Liang,Jason Wei,Andrew Y. Ng,Luke Zettlemoyer,Yejin Choi,Mike Lewis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Prefix Sliding方法，通过保留前缀指令和最近推理窗口，丢弃中间不重要token，实现无需训练即可3倍加速，或经强化学习训练后支持超长推理轨迹的高效测试时扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长程推理任务中因全注意力机制导致内存成本过高问题；发现中间推理token重要性随推理进行而降低，质疑保留全部token的必要性。

Method: 提出Prefix Sliding策略：在推理过程中丢弃既不属于前缀（关键指令/工具）也不属于最近几千token窗口的中间token；前缀保持上下文关键信息，最近窗口维持当前推理状态；支持无训练直接应用及基于强化学习的微调。

Result: 1. 无训练场景：现有模型推理速度提升3倍，性能保持不变；2. 有训练场景：结合强化学习可突破百千token限制，实现更优性能；3. 消融实验显示该方法优于中间token摘要或普通滑动窗口方法。

Conclusion: Prefix Sliding通过选择性保留关键token有效降低内存开销，为高效长程测试时扩展提供可行方案，兼具即时提速与长期性能优化潜力。

Abstract: Test-time scaling uses extra test-time compute to improve performance, such as letting language models reason longer when solving a problem. As models keep the entire reasoning trace in memory via full attention, hard tasks that need long thinking can be prohibitively expensive. However, we find most intermediate reasoning tokens lose importance as the model continues reasoning. This calls into question whether retaining them is worth the cost. Based on this insight, we propose Prefix Sliding, which discards tokens during reasoning that are not part of the prefix or the window of the last few thousand tokens. The prefix has key instructions and tools available to the model, while the most recent tokens are the current reasoning the model is working on. This caps the total memory requirement regardless of how long the model reasons, allowing for efficient long-horizon test-time scaling. Without training, Prefix Sliding can make existing models 3x faster while maintaining performance. Training with Prefix Sliding using reinforcement learning can achieve better performance by enabling scaling to reasoning traces beyond a hundred thousand tokens. Ablations show Prefix Sliding outperforms summarizing intermediate tokens or vanilla sliding window. Our code is at https://github.com/Muennighoff/prefix-sliding

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [160] [Analyzing and Reducing Search Quality Differences in Vector Similarity Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25185)
*Sara Mahdizadeh Shahri,Martin Prammer,Jignesh M. Patel,Akshitha Sriraman*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对近似最近邻搜索中“平均召回率”优化掩盖查询间差异的问题，提出RCheck运行时系统。通过动态调整搜索努力程度以平衡各查询的召回率，在保持高吞吐量的同时显著提升整体召回质量并减少资源浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据库服务通过近似最近邻搜索提升性能，但仅优化平均召回率会掩盖不同查询间召回率的显著差异。这导致部分查询召回率低于目标（损害用户体验和收入），而另一些高于目标（造成计算资源浪费）。因此，亟需检测并减少查询间的召回率差异。

Method: 设计RCheck，一种轻量级运行时系统。其核心原理是动态、高效地适应搜索努力：对低于目标召回率的查询增加搜索努力，对高于目标的则减少努力。通过调整可用的搜索努力参数来实现这一目标，便于部署。

Result: 在pgvector生产风格数据库上的评估显示，与最先进的全局调优配置相比，在相同吞吐量下，RCheck将平均召回率提高了11-93%，并使8-47%更多的查询达到目标召回率。

Conclusion: RCheck能有效解决近似搜索中召回率分布不均的问题，通过动态调整搜索策略，在保证高吞吐量的同时，显著提升了搜索质量的公平性和效率。

Abstract: Modern database services scalably search over large data collections via Approximate Nearest Neighbor Search, which improves search performance at the cost of search quality, measured by recall. In practice, a database operator seeks to achieve a target mean recall while maximizing throughput across search queries. We show that optimizing for mean recall masks significant differences in recall across queries even when target recall is met. As a result, numerous queries face (1) below-target recall, hurting user experience and revenue and (2) above-target recall, wasting computation to deliver unnecessarily high search quality. Thus, it is critical to detect and reduce recall differences across queries. We design RCheck, a light-weight run-time system that identifies low-recall queries and reduces recall differences while achieving high throughput. RCheck's key design principle is to dynamically, efficiently adapt search effort by increasing effort for queries below target recall and decreasing effort for those above it. RCheck tunes available search effort parameters, making it readily deployable. We evaluate RCheck using the widely-used production-style pgvector database. At the same throughput, RCheck improves mean recall by 11-93% and enables 8-47% more queries to meet target recall compared to the state-of-the-art globally-tuned configuration.

</details>


### [161] [Bolt-on, Verifiable Provenance for LLM-Powered Data Processing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25210)
*Yiming Lin,Sepanta Zeighami,Aditya G. Parameswaran*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出BLIP框架，通过“可验证溯源”和“最小性”概念，高效地为LLM数据处理任务提供准确且紧凑的答案来源证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM作为黑盒缺乏可信度指示，传统启发式方法（如嵌入相似度）无法保证答案来源的准确性与可靠性。

Method: 提出BLIP框架，引入“可验证溯源”（子集输入能复现原答案）和“最小性”概念；设计八种保证找到最小可验证溯源的策略及一种自适应组合策略以降低成本。

Result: 在七个数据集上实验表明，BLIP生成的溯源始终能复现答案，准确率比最佳基线高出30%以上，且成本与原查询相当。

Conclusion: BLIP提供了一种高效、低成本且保证准确性的机制，用于推断LLM数据处理任务的最小可验证溯源。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are powerful tools for processing data. However, LLMs are also complex black-boxes, returning answers to queries on data,
  without any indication for where the answer came from or whether it is trustworthy. We introduce the notion of provenance for data processing with LLMs. While
  existing heuristics (such as embedding similarity or directly asking an LLM) could provide some hints for where the answer was derived, they provide no guarantees
  that the answer can be derived using the identified provenance, and indeed, are often incorrect. Instead, we propose the notion of verifiable provenance wherein we
  identify a subset of the input text that reproduces the same (or equivalent) answer as that on the complete text, and introduce the notion of minimality, where the
  verifiable provenance is as small as possible. To identify such a provenance, a naive solution would require checking all possible subsets of the source data with the
  LLM, which is prohibitively expensive. We present BLIP, a bolt-on framework for efficiently inferring a small-sized verifiable provenance for any LLM-powered data
  processing task, with any LLM. As part of BLIP, we introduce eight strategies, each guaranteed to find a minimal verifiable provenance, as well as an adaptive
  strategy that combines their strengths to reduce cost further. We further extend BLIP to produce multiple minimal verifiable provenances. Experiments on seven
  datasets show that the provenance generated by BLIP is always guaranteed to reproduce the answer - achieving over 30% higher accuracy than the best-performing
  baseline with a comparable provenance size. Moreover, BLIP incurs a low cost, comparable to the original query on the original data.

</details>


### [162] [DBcover: A White-box SQL Test Generation Framework for Coverage Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25573)
*Yankai Rong,Shuang Liu,Jinhao Dong,Qiang Yin,Wei Lu,Jianhua Wang,Xiaoyong Du*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出DBcover，一种基于LLM和上下文推理的白盒SQL测试生成框架，通过动态分析提取SQL与执行路径的对应关系，构建统一知识图谱引导LLM生成测试用例，显著提升PostgreSQL、MySQL及KingbaseES的代码覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统模糊测试依赖随机SQL生成，无法捕捉SQL输入与内部执行路径的关联；符号执行则面临高昂成本和可扩展性限制，导致RDBMS高覆盖率测试难以实现。

Method: 1. 利用轻量级动态分析提取SQL到路径的对应关系及调用图作为全局上下文，收集目标函数周围的源码信息作为局部上下文；2. 将上述上下文组织为统一的知识图谱以支持高效检索与复用；3. 实施两阶段测试生成：首先选择语义相关且执行路径接近未覆盖目标的种子，随后结合全局与局部上下文引导LLM生成触发未覆盖代码区域的SQL测试用例。

Result: 在PostgreSQL上达到80.1%的代码覆盖率，在MySQL上达到82.3%，并在企业级闭源数据库KingbaseES上验证了有效性。

Conclusion: DBcover框架能有效解决RDBMS测试中的覆盖率瓶颈，具备对开源及闭源系统的实际适用性。

Abstract: Relational Database Management Systems (RDBMSs) are the backbone of modern data-intensive applications, making reliability and robustness critical. However, achieving high coverage in RDBMS testing remains challenging because of large codebases and complex execution logic. Traditional fuzzing relies on random SQL generation and cannot capture the correspondence between SQL inputs and internal execution paths, while symbolic execution suffers from prohibitive cost and scalability limitations.
  We propose DBcover, an LLM-driven white-box SQL test generation framework based on contextual reasoning. DBcover uses lightweight dynamic analysis to extract SQL-to-path correspondence and call graphs as global context, and collects source-level information around target functions as local context. These contexts are organized in a unified knowledge graph for efficient retrieval and reuse. DBcover then performs two-phase test generation: it first selects a semantically relevant seed whose execution path is close to the uncovered target, and then guides the LLM with global and local context to generate SQL test cases that trigger previously uncovered code regions. Experiments show that DBcover achieves 80.1% and 82.3% coverage on PostgreSQL and MySQL, and is also effective on the enterprise RDBMS KingbaseES, demonstrating its practical applicability to closed-source systems.

</details>


### [163] [PolyMemDB: A Polyglot Database System for AI Memory Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25577)
*Yu Wang,Jiaheng Lu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PolyMemDB，一个针对个人智能体记忆管理的新系统。它采用多态存储架构处理多维数据，并通过概率推理引擎解决长期事实冲突，减少大模型幻觉，提供细粒度的数据溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着个人智能体的普及，用户产生大量异构数据用于长期记忆以降低成本和提供个性化体验。然而，现有记忆系统存在两大局限：一是依赖单一存储范式导致多维数据碎片化；二是缺乏细粒度数据溯源机制，无法有效解决长期事实冲突，从而加剧大语言模型（LLM）的幻觉问题。

Method: 提出PolyMemDB系统，具备以下核心特性：1. 多态存储架构（Polyglot storage architecture），能够追踪和管理图、向量、概率及时空数据等多种类型的记忆；2. 概率推理引擎（Probabilistic inference engine），结合时间衰减与半环聚合技术，用于解析长期事实冲突并提供详细的数据溯源及透明的推理链追溯能力。

Result: 该系统成功实现了对多种类型记忆数据的统一管理，通过引入时间衰减和半环聚合的概率推理机制，有效解决了长期事实冲突问题，提供了细粒度的数据溯源功能，并增强了推理过程的透明度。

Conclusion: PolyMemDB通过其独特的多态存储和概率推理机制，克服了现有记忆系统在数据存储碎片化和事实一致性方面的不足，为智能体提供了更可靠、可解释且低幻觉的记忆管理能力。

Abstract: With the widespread adoption of personal intelligent agents, users generate massive, heterogeneous data during long-term interactions. Leveraging this data as long-term memory helps reduce token overhead and deliver personalized experiences. However, existing memory systems face two primary limitations: they rely on single-storage paradigms that fragment multi-dimensional data, and they lack fine-grained data provenance to resolve long-term factual conflicts, thereby worsening LLM hallucinations.
  In this demonstration, we introduce PolyMemDB, a novel system tailored for managing agent memory. PolyMemDB has a polyglot storage architecture designed to track and manage various memory types, including graph, vector, probability and spatial-temporal data. To ensure factual consistency and reduce hallucinations, it features a probabilistic inference engine that integrates temporal decay with semiring aggregation, resolving long-term factual conflicts, providing detailed data provenance, and enabling users to trace reasoning chains transparently.

</details>


### [164] [MetaSieve: Faster Relational Deep Learning through SQL-Based Metapath Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25903)
*Fahim Shahriar Khan,Ashraf Aboulnaga*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出MetaSieve，通过SQL连接和聚合查询筛选有效元路径，剪枝冗余路径，从而显著降低关系图神经网络训练的子图规模和耗时，同时保持或提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关系深度学习（RDL）中，基于图神经网络的训练成本主要取决于采样子图的大小。现有的方法往往包含大量冗余的元路径，导致计算开销巨大。

Method: 提出MetaSieve层，利用关系数据库系统的连接和聚合能力。通过SQL查询计算候选元路径扩展的统计信息，并使用新颖的评分函数评估其信息量与轻量级程度。低于阈值的元路径被判定为无信息并剪枝，该过程独立于GNN参数。

Result: 在RelBench基准测试上，结合多种GNN骨干网络进行评估，结果显示MetaSieve能大幅减少每个epoch的训练时间，同时维持甚至提高了模型的准确性。

Conclusion: MetaSieve提供了一种高效、与GNN架构无关的元路径选择机制，证明了利用数据库原生能力优化RDL训练效率的可行性。

Abstract: Relational Deep Learning (RDL) is an effective approach to machine learning over multi-table relational databases. In RDL, a database is modeled as a graph in which each row is a node and each foreign-key relation is an edge, and a graph neural network (GNN) is trained on this graph. Training a GNN requires sampling a subgraph around every seed node in the training set, and the cost of training is largely determined by the size of these subgraphs. This paper aims to reduce subgraph size by leveraging the join and aggregation capabilities of relational database systems. We observe that sampled subgraphs are obtained by following metapaths composed of foreign-key links, and that many of these metapaths can be pruned without loss of accuracy. We present MetaSieve, a metapath selection layer that determines which metapaths to retain and which to prune. For each candidate metapath extension, MetaSieve computes statistics via SQL join and aggregation queries and evaluates the extension based on a novel scoring function that prefers lightweight but informative candidates. Metapaths whose scores fall below a threshold are deemed uninformative and pruned. Metapath selection in MetaSieve is lightweight since it relies only on database statistics and task labels, and it is independent of GNN parameters, so it integrates with diverse GNN architectures for classification and regression. Our evaluation on the RelBench benchmark with multiple GNN backbones shows that MetaSieve consistently reduces per-epoch training time by large margins while maintaining and often improving accuracy.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [165] [SWIM: Step-Wise Integrated Measure for Session-supervised List Evaluation in Generative Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25104)
*Yuanhao Pu,Chenghao Zhang,Chao Feng,Xunyong Yang,Xiang Li,Yongqi Liu,Defu Lian,Kaiqiao Zhan,Kun Gai*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出SWIM，一种基于生存过程建模的列表级评估器，解决传统G-E框架中忽略序列依赖的问题，显著提升推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Generator-Evaluator (G-E) 框架中的评估器通过聚合点级分数打分，隐含假设曝光独立性，无法捕捉连续消费场景下的上下文依赖、用户持续行为及重复内容的边际效用递减等会话级动态。

Method: 提出SWIM（Step-Wise Integrated Measure），将用户行为建模为有限视界前缀会话级生存过程；利用因果掩码Transformer并行估计续存概率和条件奖励，分解并估算当前列表对会话目标的贡献。

Result: 在列表重排序任务中，SWIM显著优于基线方法，大幅提升了整体推荐参与度指标。

Conclusion: SWIM有效解决了传统评估器忽略序列动态的问题，满足了工业界严格的延迟要求，并在实际应用中表现出优越的性能。

Abstract: Modern industrial recommender systems have increasingly adopted the Generator-Evaluator (G-E) framework for the re-ranking stage. Within this paradigm, the generator produces candidate item lists from a pool filtered by upstream retrieval and ranking modules, while the evaluator scores these lists and selects the highest-scoring one for final exposure per request. However, on sequential platforms (e.g., short-video apps), users consume items continuously, ignoring artificial list boundaries. Conventional evaluators score lists by aggregating point-wise values, implicitly assuming exposure independence. This fails to capture critical session-level dynamics, such as contextual dependencies, user continuation, and diminishing marginal utility from repetitive content.
  To bridge this gap, we propose SWIM (Step-Wise Integrated Measure), a list-level evaluator that models user behaviors as a finite-horizon prefix session-level survival process. SWIM estimates the prefix-conditioned contribution of the current list to the session-level objective by factorizing it into a recursive survival distribution and reached-position conditional rewards. Leveraging a causally-masked Transformer, SWIM efficiently estimates continuation probabilities and utilities in parallel, satisfying strict industrial latency constraints. Extensive experiments demonstrate that SWIM significantly outperforms baselines in listwise reranking tasks, yielding substantial improvements in overall recommendation engagement.

</details>


### [166] [The "Curse of Knowledge" in LLM Query Simulation: Concept Provenance for Tracing Answer-Side Intrusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25245)
*Chenglong Ma,Xinye Wanyan,Danula Hettiachchi,Ziqi Xu,Jeffrey Chan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出“概念溯源”框架，用于检测和区分LLM生成搜索查询中的答案侧知识侵入，发现该现象普遍存在但仅占评估方差的极小部分，主要作为合规性诊断工具而非评估预测指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索评估中广泛使用LLM生成的搜索查询，但这些查询可能包含预设答案知识的概念，违反了预搜索用户的信息访问边界。现有的验证指标（如重叠度、多样性、有效性）无法区分罕见的人类尾部变异与候选答案侧的侵入。

Method: 引入“概念溯源”（Concept Provenance）框架，将查询概念分配到支持背景故事、以人为中心、人类尾部和候选答案侧四个区域。在100个UQV100主题、8个LLM和5种提示条件下，对77,004个查询应用该框架，并通过两种提取管道进行验证。

Result: 跨管道token-HCIR Spearman rho为1.0；候选答案侧概念占非通用概念的7.40%，出现在97/100的主题中，解释了约67%的方差；人工验证显示68.2%的松弛精度，揭示知识侵入（45.5%）和部署侵入（45.0%）两种机制；删除效应量d=-0.47高于随机删除的-0.34，但这些概念仅解释不到2%的聚合评估方差。

Conclusion: 概念溯源主要用作边界合规性的诊断工具，而非评估转移的预测器。在当前测试条件下，没有提示条件能消除侵入；但通过生成后的概念溯源选择可实现99%的侵入消除。

Abstract: LLM-generated search queries are widely used to augment IR evaluation, yet they may contain concepts that presuppose answer-side document knowledge, violating the information-access boundary of pre-search users. Existing validation metrics, including overlap, diversity, and effectiveness, cannot distinguish rare human-tail variation from candidate answer-side intrusion. We introduce concept provenance, a framework that assigns query concepts to backstory-supported, human-central, human-tail, and candidate answer-side zones, operationalizing a boundary that retrieval metrics alone cannot detect. Applying concept provenance to 77,004 queries across 100 UQV100 topics, 8 LLMs, and 5 prompt conditions with two extraction pipelines, we obtain a cross-pipeline token-HCIR Spearman rho of 1.0 over five condition means. Candidate answer-side concepts constitute 7.40 percent of non-generic concepts and appear in 97 of 100 topics, with topic explaining approximately 67 percent of variance. Human validation yields 68.2 percent relaxed precision, revealing two mechanisms: knowledge intrusion at 45.5 percent and deployment intrusion at 45.0 percent. Diagnostic probes show disproportionate localized retrieval effects, with deletion effect size d = -0.47 compared with d = -0.34 for random deletion, but these concepts explain less than 2 percent of aggregate evaluation variance. Concept provenance therefore serves as a boundary-compliance diagnostic rather than an evaluation-shift predictor. Under the tested conditions, no prompt condition eliminates intrusion; post-generation concept-provenance selection achieves 99 percent elimination.

</details>


### [167] [Rank-Deviation Quality: A Distance-Aware Metric for Multi-Answer Retrieval and Ranking Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25318)
*Xiaokun Zhou,Alessandro Moschitti,Danielle Class*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出一种名为RDQ的检索评估指标，适应不同参考项数量的查询，结合位置权重与秩偏差惩罚，在POI数据集上表现出比现有指标更高的统计功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估指标难以适应具有不同数量有效结果的查询，且多数指标依赖绝对相关性等级或仅关注排序一致性，缺乏对“哪些项目被检索”及“如何排序”的综合考量，同时标注成本较高。

Method: 引入Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ)指标，基于有序参考列表（ORL），计算每个检索到的参考项的输出位置权重与秩偏差惩罚的乘积，非参考项得零分；通过参数控制对错误排序的容忍度，支持成对或列表式判断生成的序数排名数据。

Result: 在5000个查询的POI数据集上，RDQ在13种配置中具有最高的中位empirical power@100；达到与完整查询排序均值tau>=0.8一致仅需200个查询，优于最强非RDQ配置（250个查询）；在TREC Deep Learning基准测试中，RDQ在n=25时达到与NDCG相当的中位功效，但在n=100时NDCG表现更高。

Conclusion: RDQ是一种高效、灵活的检索评估指标，能有效平衡检索召回与排序质量，降低标注要求，并在多数场景下展现出优于传统指标的统计敏感性和实用性。

Abstract: We introduce Rank-Deviation Quality (RDQ), an evaluation metric for retrieval and ranking systems that adapts to queries with varying numbers of reference items, from a single correct answer to many valid results. RDQ scores a candidate ranking against an ordered reference list (ORL): each retrieved reference item contributes its output-position weight multiplied by a rank-deviation penalty, and items outside the ORL receive zero credit. Application-specific parameters control tolerance to misordering. Larger values emphasize retrieving valid reference items, whereas smaller values place more weight on matching their reference order. The output-position weights can reflect visibility in the application's interface, such as a vertical list or a carousel. Unlike metrics that require absolute relevance grades, RDQ operates on ordinal rankings, which annotators can produce through pairwise or listwise judgments. Unlike rank-correlation measures such as Kendall's tau, RDQ accounts for both which items are returned and how they are ordered. On a 5,000-query point-of-interest (POI) dataset with 12 systems, RDQ has the highest median empirical power@100 among the 13 evaluated metric configurations. It reaches mean tau >= 0.8 agreement with its own full-query ordering at 200 queries; RBP(0.9), the strongest tested non-RDQ configuration, reaches the same threshold at 250. On TREC Deep Learning benchmarks, where NDCG uses native graded labels and RDQ uses ordinal tiers derived from them, RDQ reaches comparable median power at n=25, while NDCG is higher at n=100.

</details>


### [168] [CRAMER: Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25370)
*Zhiyuan Julian Su,Naihe Feng,Zhen Luther Qin,Ga Wu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出CRAMER框架，通过自然语言请求对冻结的推荐模型进行掩码控制，实现即时、低成本的个性化推荐适配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有顺序推荐模型对用户即时请求响应灵活性不足的问题；克服现有适配方法因全量重训练或依赖大语言模型推理而导致的高计算开销，从而适用于大规模推荐服务。

Method: 基于模型控制理论，将用户自然语言请求视为控制信号，通过掩码机制（masking）调制冻结的主干网络参数，实现无需重训练的即时行为调整。

Result: 在多个大规模基准数据集上，CRAMER在多项推荐指标上优于四种最先进的相关基线方法，同时保持极低的计算开销；具备增强的可控性和跨领域适应能力。

Conclusion: CRAMER为请求感知的顺序推荐建立了新范式，证明了通过掩码控制实现高效、灵活的用户请求适配的可行性与优越性。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation models, while powerful, have limited flexibility in responding to immediate user requests, making it difficult to adapt their recommendations to the user's timely interests. Unfortunately, existing user request adaptation methods often incur high computational overhead due to either 1) retraining the entire backbone network or 2) leveraging the inference ability of large language models (a.k.a. prompt engineering), limiting their applicability in large-scale recommendation services. This paper presents Control via Request-Aware Masking for Editing Recommenders (CRAMER), a framework that takes users' natural-language requests to immediately change sequential recommendation models' behavior. Specifically, inspired by the model control theory, CRAMER treats user requests as control signals to modulate frozen backbone parameters through masking, achieving instant adaptation to diverse requests while avoiding costly retraining. Experiments on multiple large-scale benchmark datasets show that CRAMER outperforms four state-of-the-art request-aware baselines across multiple recommendation metrics while achieving minimal overhead. Moreover, the proposed framework exhibits enhanced controllability and cross-domain adaptability, establishing a new paradigm for request-aware sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [169] [MOTIF: Motivation-guided Topology Inference for Cold-start Multimodal Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25381)
*Yurui Shi,Yuchen Miao,Ximing Hu,Zijun Wang,Chang Han*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出MOTIF框架，通过离线LLM推理动机语义、重构知识增强图拓扑及加权对比学习，解决冷启动多模态推荐中的稀疏交互、孤立物品和语义漂移问题，在三个基准上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决冷启动多模态推荐面临的三大耦合挑战：(i) 稀疏交互模糊用户意图，(ii) 冷启动物品在拓扑上孤立，(iii) 基于相似度的物品图可能导致语义漂移。

Method: 提出MOTIF（Motivation-guided Topology Inference）框架，包含四个核心模块：语义动机推理、知识增强图重构、加权图对比学习和语义-结构对齐。利用离线大语言模型（LLM）推理推断动机语义，重构可迁移的物品-物品拓扑，并在不将生成文本注入预测过程的情况下学习鲁棒的图嵌入。

Result: 在三个多模态基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法一致地优于基于图、多模态、冷启动及LLM增强的基线方法，相比最强的近期基线，相对提升高达6.07%。

Conclusion: MOTIF框架通过结合LLM动机推理与图拓扑重构，有效解决了冷启动场景下的推荐难题，提升了多模态推荐的性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Cold-start multimodal recommendation faces three coupled challenges: (i) sparse interactions obscure user intent, (ii) cold items remain topologically isolated, and (iii) similarity-based item graphs may cause semantic drift. To address these issues, we propose MOTIF, a Motivation-guided Topology Inference framework for cold-start multimodal recommendation. MOTIF integrates Semantic Motivation Reasoning, Knowledge-enhanced Graph Reconstruction, Weighted Graph Contrastive Learning, and Semantic-Structural Alignment. It uses offline LLM reasoning to infer motivation semantics, reconstructs transferable item-item topology, and learns robust graph embeddings without injecting generated text into prediction. Experiments on three multimodal benchmarks show consistent gains over graph-based, multimodal, cold-start, and LLM-enhanced baselines, with up to 6.07% relative improvement over the strongest recent baseline.

</details>


### [170] [DocPC: Document-Level Visual Retrieval via Representative Page Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25434)
*Chengsong You,Junwei Zhou,Nan Du*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出DocPC框架，通过选取代表性页面并组合成单张网格图像进行文档级视觉检索，显著降低索引成本（存储减少10.1倍，时间减少7.7倍），并在DocViRe基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉文档检索方法多为页面中心主义，无法有效应对现实场景中需要完整文档检索的需求；简单的“先页面后文档”聚合方式存在线性索引成本高、跨页相关性导致检索性能下降的问题。

Method: 提出基于代表性页面组成（Representative Page Composition）的DocPC框架：1) 选择代表性页面并拼接为单张网格图像以进行文档级索引；2) 结合多正样本对比学习与稀疏调度的列表式优化处理多正样本监督；3) 发布包含多正样本相关性的DocViRe基准测试集。

Result: DocPC-ColQwen在DocViRe基准上NDCG@5达到44.09，优于最强页面级基线（38.91），同时将存储需求降低10.1倍，端到端索引时间缩短约7.7倍。

Conclusion: DocPC通过文档级视觉检索框架有效解决了跨页相关性检索难题，在提升检索性能的同时大幅降低了计算和存储资源消耗，为文档级视觉检索提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Visual document retrieval has advanced by encoding page screenshots with vision-language models, bypassing OCR pipelines. However, existing methods remain page-centric, misaligned with real-world scenarios requiring complete document retrieval. A naive page-then-document aggregation suffers from linear indexing cost and degraded retrieval when relevance spans multiple pages. We propose DocPC, a document-level visual retrieval framework based on Representative Page Composition: selecting representative pages and composing them into a single grid image for document-level indexing, reducing indexed images, vectors, and storage by 10.1x and end-to-end indexing time by roughly 7.7x. To handle multi-positive supervision prevalent at the document level, we combine multi-positive contrastive learning with sparsely scheduled listwise optimization. We also introduce DocViRe, a benchmark with multi-positive relevance annotations. DocPC-ColQwen achieves NDCG@5 of 44.09 on DocViRe, outperforming the strongest page-level baseline at 38.91 while reducing storage by 10.1x. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/DocPC-Document-Level-Visual-Retrieval-via-Representative-Page-Composition-1D52. Data is available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/anonymous-7219/docpc.

</details>


### [171] [Query Expansion Is More Than Generation: Improving Dense Retrieval through Better Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25521)
*Siyuan Sun,Mihai Surdeanu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AnchorQE，一种无需训练的查询扩展方法，通过分别编码原始查询和扩展文本并动态插值，解决大语言模型生成的扩展文本导致稠密检索器性能下降的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管大语言模型（LLMs）能生成查询扩展，但这些扩展常使冻结的稠密检索器性能变差。现有研究多关注生成内容本身，忽视了扩展文本如何融入检索器的系统性问题。

Method: 引入AnchorQE方法：1. 将原始查询和其扩展文本分别编码；2. 采用无监督在线策略估计插值因子，仅在未标记测试流的小部分上操作；3. 当扩展文本既具有强检索能力又与原始查询的检索证据一致时，赋予高信任度。

Result: 在TREC-DL、LoTTE和BEIR数据集上，相比广泛使用的仅扩展或文本级拼接基线，检索有效性提升高达12.89%；其在线估计插值因子的策略优于在开发集上调优的固定权重，性能提升最高达3.81%。

Conclusion: 查询扩展的性能退化主要源于集成方法而非生成内容本身。通过分离编码和基于一致性的动态插值，可有效利用LLM生成的扩展文本而不损害检索性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate query expansions without task-specific training, yet the same expansions often make a frozen dense retriever worse. We identify an underexplored factor: prior work has often focused on what text is generated, while how generated text is incorporated into dense retrievers has received less systematic attention. By holding generated expansions fixed, we show that performance degradation can often be attributed to the integration method itself. We introduce AnchorQE, a training-free method that separately encodes the original query and its expansion before interpolating them. The interpolation factor is estimated using an unsupervised online strategy that operates over a small part of the unlabeled test stream. Intuitively, our strategy assigns high expansion trust only when expansions are both retrieval-strong and consistent with the original query's retrieved evidence. We show that AnchorQE improves retrieval effectiveness by up to 12.89% when compared to widely-used expansion-only or text-level concatenation baselines across TREC-DL, LoTTE, and BEIR. Further, we show that our online strategy to estimate the interpolation factor outperforms a fixed weight tuned on a development partition by up to 3.81%.

</details>


### [172] [TransRetrieval: Scaling Up Transformer-Based Retrieval for Industrial Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25528)
*Zhifei Zheng,Yunfei Liu,Bin Liu,Qiren Zhu,Hanbing Liu,Ziru Xu,Han Zhu,Jian Xu,Qi Qi,Bo Zheng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出TransRetrieval框架，通过加权平均聚合、目标Token压缩和位置风格域嵌入解决推荐检索中的特征异构问题，实现计算预算与跨域数据的可扩展性。在工业数据和KuaiRand基准上验证了鲁棒的线性缩放效果，线上A/B测试提升收入2.53%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将缩放定律应用于推荐检索面临特征异构挑战：简单堆叠Transformer层因异构字段导致严重的Token范数发散，从而产生收益递减效应。

Method: 1) 加权平均聚合：恢复Transformer依赖的同质Token假设；2) 目标Token压缩：在保持交叉注意力表达能力同时减少85%每候选FLOPs；3) 位置风格域嵌入：以极低代价统一多领域，使跨域数据成为缩放资产。

Result: 在400亿交互的工业数据集和KuaiRand基准上，将每个目标的计算量从0.1增至2 MFLOPs，Recall@2000提升19.3/22.2个百分点，证实了对数线性缩放特性。

Conclusion: TransRetrieval有效解决了推荐检索中的特征异构问题，实现了计算资源的高效利用，并在保证端到端延迟不变的前提下显著提升了平台收入和检索性能。

Abstract: Applying scaling laws to recommendation retrieval is hindered by feature heterogeneity: naively stacking Transformer layers yields diminishing returns because heterogeneous fields produce severe token-norm divergence. We present TransRetrieval, a Transformer-based retrieval framework that scales with both computational budget and cross-domain data. The key enabler is (1) weighted average aggregation, which restores the homogeneous-token assumption Transformers rely on. Building on this, we introduce (2) target token compression that cuts per-candidate FLOPs by 85% while preserving cross-attention expressiveness, and (3) position-style domain embeddings that unify multiple domains at negligible additional cost, turning cross-domain data into a scaling asset. On a 40-billion-interaction industrial dataset and the public KuaiRand benchmark, scaling compute from 0.1 to 2 MFLOPs per target yields +19.3/+22.2 pt Recall@2000, confirming robust log-linear scaling. In online A/B tests, TransRetrieval lifts platform revenue by 2.53% under the same end-to-end latency constraint as the production baseline.

</details>


### [173] [An Event is Worth One Token: Event Tokenization for Industrial-scale LLM Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25546)
*Fan Xia,Zhaoheng Zheng,Iman Setayesh,Ruogu Lin,Yiqin Pan,Samarth Mittal,Wentao Bao,Vinti Pandey,Sachin Patil,Jianpeng Cheng,Jun Xiao,Zhuang Wang,Xiangjun Fan,Sri Reddy,Minghai Chen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出AMBER框架，通过“事件中心”范式和高解析度时间快照（Event Token）解决LLM推荐中信息丢失问题，显著提升推荐性能并优化计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的推荐系统仅对每个位置编码文本、语义ID或少数分类特征，丢弃了丰富的用户、物品、上下文和结果信号；自回归建模导致查询信号弱且误差累积。

Method: 提出事件中心范式，将每次交互表示为完整的时间快照；引入“快照分辨率”作为新扩展维度；提出AMBER模型，端到端学习压缩快照为紧凑的“Event Token”，预计算缓存以解耦推理算力与分辨率扩展。

Result: 在工业级基准测试中，AMBER优于其他推荐范式；统一tokenizer表现优于专用tokenizer；Event Tokens可迁移至非LLM排序器带来显著改进；增加Event Tokenizer容量进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: AMBER通过高解析度事件表征有效提升了LLM推荐的精度与效率，证明了跨架构迁移能力及进一步扩展的潜力。

Abstract: LLM-based recommendation has scaled along model capacity and sequence length, yet each position encodes only text, semantic IDs, or a few categorical features, discarding rich user, item, context, and outcome signals available at each event. Under autoregressive modeling, this yields weak queries at each position and, since each position becomes context for the next, the degradation compounds across the sequence. We propose an event-centric paradigm that represents each interaction by its full temporal snapshot, and identify a new scaling dimension we term snapshot resolution: the amount of information encoded per event. To efficiently scale snapshot resolution, we introduce AMBER (Autoregressive Modeling via Bottlenecked Event Representation), which compresses each temporal snapshot into a compact Event Token, a new LLM input modality. The representation is learned end-to-end, while Event Tokens are pre-computed and cached for serving, decoupling snapshot resolution from real-time serving compute. On industrial-scale ranking and retrieval benchmarks, AMBER advances the compute-quality Pareto frontier relative to alternative recommendation paradigms. At sufficient capacity, a single unified tokenizer even outperforms dedicated per-entity tokenizers, demonstrating positive transfer across structurally different entity types. AMBER's Event Tokens also transfer across model architectures: when integrated into a heavily optimized non-LLM ranker as serving-time historical features, they yield statistically significant improvements. Further scaling Event Tokenizer capacity provides additional improvements.

</details>


### [174] [When Stale Constraints Go Unchecked: Budgeted Verification Failures in Inherited Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25553)
*Kazuki Nakayashiki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究在有限验证预算下，智能体能否从继承的旧记忆中恢复已撤销的约束。发现仅靠自然分配会导致大量过时决策，但将部分资源重新分配给关键溯源路径可显著提升准确性，表明内存系统需独立的时效性信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决智能体继承整合记忆时可能面临的“继承过时约束”问题，即在权威记录更新后，旧约束仍被错误使用，且在验证预算稀缺的情况下如何避免此类错误。

Method: 显式建模替代关系（历史溯源不可变，当前记录可变），设定固定的两项记录验证预算。通过对比“原生分配”与“将一槽位重新分配至关键溯源路径或随机记录”两种策略，评估智能体在不同场景下的决策一致性。

Result: 原生分配下，当约束被替代时，智能体产生过时一致决策的比例高达74.7%-77.3%。将一槽位重分配至关键溯源路径后，当前记录一致决策率显著提升了61.3-74.0个百分点，且该干预在所有模型和重复实验中均表现正向有效。

Conclusion: 由于过时记忆错误主要由验证分配结构限制导致，接近其结构性上限，因此现有的相关性机制不足以处理时效性问题。内存系统需要独立于相关性的“新鲜度”或“替代”信号来确保决策的时效准确性。

Abstract: An agent that inherits a consolidated memory may inherit a constraint that was true when written and has since been withdrawn by a newer authoritative record. Under a scarce verification budget, does the agent recover the withdrawal, and if not, is the error avoidable without spending more? We model supersession explicitly -- historical provenance is immutable; what changes is which record is current -- and assign by design the memory's form, the world's state (source current or superseded), and the verification policy at a fixed budget of two records: the agent's own allocation, or the same budget with one slot re-assigned to the critical provenance path or to a random record. With a constraint stated, agents inspected its provenance path in about one episode in five; when that constraint had been superseded, native allocation produced stale-consistent decisions in 77.3%, 74.7% and 74.7% of episodes across a primary run, a fresh-wording replication and a held-out domain. Re-assigning one slot to the critical path raised current-record-consistent decisions by +74.0, +72.7 and +61.3 points, positive in six of six models in each of those runs, and changed nothing when the record agreed with the memory. The held-out scenario was later found to contain a temporal inconsistency; a robustness replication with one sentence corrected, deposited externally before execution, gave +73.3 points and is reported alongside the original. The intervention uses knowledge of the critical path and is not a scheduler; it identifies that the share of stale-memory error attributable to verification allocation is close to its structural ceiling. Memory systems may need freshness or supersession signals separate from relevance.

</details>


### [175] [RetrievalRouter: Joint Modality and Architecture Selection for Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25625)
*Emre Kuru,Mehmet Onur Keskin,Reza Farahbakhsh,Noel Crespi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出RetrievalRouter，一种基于查询文本的轻量级自适应路由机制，动态选择最优检索管道，在保持高精度的同时显著提升速度，解决了传统静态管道在精度与延迟间的权衡难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代检索系统在金融、医疗和法律等高 stakes 场景中面临严峻的“精度-延迟”权衡：高效管道难以处理复杂文档中的证据，而高效果管道因速度慢、成本高无法大规模应用。现有方法缺乏基于查询级别的动态适配依据，导致从业者只能在漏检证据和不可用延迟间被动选择。

Method: 引入RetrievalRouter，一个轻量级的查询感知路由器。该模型仅根据查询文本学习并决定最适合当前查询的检索管道（模态或架构）。通过单一可调参数，用户可探索完整的精度-延迟前沿（frontier），实现不同场景下的灵活配置。

Result: 1. 相比最佳静态基线，RetrievalRouter准确率提升2.5%，速度提高12.4倍。
2. 在多个金融和科学语料库基准测试中，无单一静态管道始终占优。
3. 相比先前的自适应策略选择方法，在精度导向设置下nDCG@5显著更高；在延迟导向设置下，精度相当或数值上更优，且延迟表现持平。

Conclusion: 无需在精度和延迟间进行硬性妥协。通过查询感知的动态路由，可以同时获得比任何静态基线更好的准确性和更快的速度。RetrievalRouter为检索系统提供了 principled（有原则/系统性）的自适应选择机制。

Abstract: Document retrieval increasingly supports high-stakes information access in finance, healthcare, and law. Modern retrieval pipelines vary both in modality (text or multimodal) and in retrieval architecture (dense or late-interaction). These choices impose a hard compromise: the most effective pipelines are too slow and expensive to run at scale, while the fastest fail to retrieve evidence from complex documents. Practitioners must therefore choose between missed evidence and unusable latency, with no principled basis for adapting that choice at the query level. We show that this compromise is unnecessary. Not every query requires the same pipeline. Across benchmarks spanning financial and scientific corpora, no static pipeline dominates. We introduce RetrievalRouter, a lightweight query-aware router that learns, from the query text alone, which retrieval pipeline best fits each query. A single tunable parameter exposes the full accuracy-latency frontier, and for every static baseline, RetrievalRouter offers an operating point that is simultaneously more accurate and faster. Against the best static baseline, RetrievalRouter is 2.5% more accurate and 12.4 times faster. Furthermore, compared with prior adaptive strategy selection methods, RetrievalRouter achieves significantly higher nDCG@5 across accuracy-oriented settings, while matching or numerically outperforming them on both nDCG@5 and latency in latency-oriented settings. Our code and data are available at https://github.com/emrekuruu/retrieval-router.

</details>


### [176] [Data Citation for Large Language Models: A Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25663)
*Gianmaria Silvello*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出大语言模型的数据引用是区别于文档引用的独立难题，提出了训练数据归因、推理时数据引用及知识图谱事实引用三个研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将引用视为文本验证手段，忽略了数据引用在信用归属和溯源方面的独特功能，且数据引用比文档引用更难解决。

Method: 分析大语言模型数据引用的特殊性，界定其在验证、溯源和信用分配上的需求，并梳理出三个关键的研究方向。

Result: 明确了三大挑战：1. 将影响估计转化为模型参数中吸收语料库的引用；2. 在推理时以适当粒度和固定性识别数据集、子集及查询结果；3. 定义单个三元组的引用含义及信用沿溯源传播机制。

Conclusion: 解决上述问题需要数据库、信息检索、知识表示和人工智能社区的跨学科合作。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly mediate access to information, and a growing body of work asks whether they cite the sources behind their outputs. That work treats citation as a verification device and applies it to textual documents. Scholarly citation serves two further functions, credit and provenance, and it applies to data as much as to text. This paper argues that data citation for large language models is an open challenge, distinct from document-level citation grounding and harder to solve. We ask how such models should cite data so that outputs stay verifiable, provenance stays traceable, and credit reaches data creators and curators. We set out three research directions. Training data attribution has to turn influence estimates into references for corpora absorbed into model parameters. Data citation at inference time has to identify datasets, subsets, and query results at the right granularity and fixity. Citing knowledge graph facts has to define what a reference to a single triple denotes and how credit propagates along provenance. Progress on all three depends on joint work across the database, information retrieval, knowledge representation, and artificial intelligence communities.

</details>


### [177] [D3ER: Supporting Multi-Modal Recommendation via Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25737)
*Bingnan Wang,Yi Li,Xiongxin Tang,Fanjiang Xu,Jiangmeng Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出D3ER方法，通过引入梯度提升和知识蒸馏，解决多模态推荐中同质/异质判别信息联合学习导致的效能削弱问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在联合学习模态同质判别信息(HOI)和模态异质判别信息(HEI)时，往往削弱了各自的有效性。

Method: 提出基于解耦与蒸馏的动态集成多模态推荐(D3ER)。首次将梯度提升引入MR以交替优化HOI和HEI；结合知识蒸馏和全局校正正则化以降低存储成本和避免局部最优。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上的实验证实了该方法在多模态推荐任务中的优越性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过专业化优化和辅助机制，有效提升了多模态推荐的性能。

Abstract: Incorporating items' information shared among multiple modalities into a fused representation, multi-modal recommendation (MR) has demonstrated documented success than canonical unimodal recommendation. Although several attempts have been made to extract the discriminative information unique in each modality, existing methods suffer from a core limitation: the joint learning of modal-homogeneity discriminative information (HOI) and modal-heterogeneity discriminative information (HEI) tends to weaken their individual effectiveness. To remedy this deficiency, we propose a novel method, dubbed Disentangle and Distillation-based Dynamic Ensemble for multi-modal Recommendation (D3ER). We introduce gradient boosting into MR for the first time to formalize the optimization objective for alternately learning HOI and HEI. This design enables models dedicated to each type of information to focus on their proficient samples, thereby promoting specialized optimization. Furthermore, to mitigate the inherent high storage cost and risk of local optima in gradient boosting, we enhance our framework with knowledge distillation and a global correction regularization. Experiments on prevalent real-world datasets confirm the superiority of our proposed method on MR.

</details>


### [178] [Hamiltonian Spectral-Temporal Dissipative Dynamics for Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25755)
*Shuiying Liao,P. Y. Mok*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出哈密顿谱推荐器(HSR)，将序列偏好演化建模为二阶动力系统，通过位置(稳定偏好)和动量(短期倾向)在潜相空间中捕捉惯性、周期性和突发波动，优于Transformer和SSM模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有序列推荐模型多基于一阶过程假设（仅依赖当前状态），无法充分捕捉用户行为中丰富的动态特性，如惯性、周期性及突发转变。

Method: 引入哈密顿谱推荐器(HSR)，将偏好演化重构为潜相空间中的耗散哈密顿系统；利用线性时不变结构的频域闭式解，结合可学习耗散机制（模拟兴趣衰减）和局部脉冲细化模块（模拟稀疏日志中的突发行为波动）。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，HSR consistently 优于最先进的基于Transformer和状态空间模型(SSM)的推荐器。

Conclusion: HSR通过二阶动力系统框架有效整合了全局周期模式、惯性演化和局部冲击，解决了现有模型对这些现象表征不足的问题，显著提升了序列推荐的性能。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation requires understanding how user preferences evolve over time, yet most existing models treat such evolution as a first order process where the next state depends solely on the current latent representation. Nevertheless, real user behavior often exhibits richer dynamics, including inertia, periodicity, and sudden shifts that cannot be fully captured by these first order assumptions. Motivated by these behavioral characteristics, we reconceptualize sequential recommendation through the lens of second order dynamical systems and introduce the Hamiltonian Spectral Recommender (HSR), which recasts preference evolution as a dissipative Hamiltonian system in a latent phase space of position (stable preference) and momentum (short-term tendency). The linear time-invariant structure of the governing equation admits a closed-form solution in the frequency domain. A learnable dissipation mechanism further captures natural interest decay, while a short local impulse refinement module models abrupt behavioral fluctuations commonly observed in sparse interaction logs. This design jointly accounts for global periodic patterns, inertial evolution, and localized shocks, where three phenomena that are underrepresented in existing sequential models. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that HSR consistently outperforms state-of-the-art Transformer-based and state space model (SSM)-based recommenders.

</details>


### [179] [PUMA: Post-Hoc Sparsification of Universal Multimodal Embeddings for Efficient Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.25780)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Franco Maria Nardini,Claudio Pomo,Cosimo Rulli,Rossano Venturini,Tommaso Di Noia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PUMA是一种用于通用多模态嵌入器的后处理稀疏化方法，通过稀疏自编码器将稠密表示映射为紧凑的稀疏代码，无需重训练骨干网络。它在保持检索性能的同时，显著降低了存储成本（8-16倍）并提升了推理速度（最高25倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用多模态嵌入器虽然支持跨文本、图像及混合查询的检索，但其稠密表示导致高昂的内存占用和推理成本。尽管事后稀疏化（post-hoc sparsification）可能降低成本，但在多模态检索领域仍缺乏深入探索。

Method: 提出PUMA框架：首先进行预训练阶段以保留稠密点积几何结构，随后微调稀疏编码器以适配检索任务。该方法无需重新训练底层骨干模型，直接作用于现有通用多模态嵌入器。

Result: 在五个涵盖文本到图像及组合图像检索的基准测试中评估Qwen3-VL-Embedding-2B模型上的表现。PUMA在四个数据集上与稠密检索统计无显著差异或优于稠密检索。同时识别出事后稀疏化的两种失败模式：Pre-TopK支持不足和检索不对齐的活动支持。

Conclusion: PUMA有效解决了多模态检索中的效率瓶颈，通过稀疏化大幅减少向量存储需求并加速大规模候选池中的精确评分，为高效多模态检索提供了可行的解决方案。
